- 2026-07-21: Every hosted demo now serves a public web ROOT page at `/` — a self-contained, CDN-free info page a human/AI-assistant visitor lands on: the demo's name + one-line service framing, live DOMAIN activity counters read server-side from that demo's OWN tables (not telemetry — e.g. skooti fleet + rides, atablefor tables/covers, hoteling rooms/nights, philslist listings, tudu lists/todos/members), and a copy-pasteable "poke it" section pointing at `/.well-known/kiosk.json` + `/kiosk/schema` + the skill. Created a root for the four API-only/proc-root demos (atablefor, skooti, hoteling, philslist, stylish) and augmented getgrocery's storefront + tudu's web UI with the stat cards.
- 2026-07-21: Hosted-demo pre-HN features — a per-demo `KIOSK_POW_DIFFICULTY` knob (default `low`, opt-in `high` maps to the heavy Equihash 168/7 for skooti + atablefor with an honest "beware: intensive PoW" banner in `/.well-known/kiosk.json`, unset = low so CI stays fast), and opt-in, app-layer, privacy-safe live-activity telemetry (a shared append-only events store + a per-demo Rack hook recording one generic event per successful wire action under a per-app-salted, non-cross-app-joinable agent hash, an aggregate `GET /demo/activity.json`, and a seed/simulate task) wired into all seven demos and gated OFF by default (`KIOSK_TELEMETRY=1`).
- 2026-07-20: Re-pinned all seven demos (and the engine default skill URL) to the immutable `skill-v0.3.1` — the "agent" → "AI assistant" terminology pass reached the skill; demo READMEs/before-after adopt the same voice, and the hoteling/skooti stub READMEs were fleshed out to match their siblings.
- 2026-07-20: Bumped the wire to protocol 0.3.0 (`API_VERSION`/`MIN_CLIENT`) — a backward-compatible feature-milestone MINOR over 0.2 (KYC, roles-from-IdP, per-assistant spending caps, the non-commerce demos) with no breaking wire change; re-pinned all seven demos to the new immutable `skill-v0.3.0`.
- 2026-07-20: Reframed the food-delivery demo into `kiosk-demo-atablefor`, a restaurant TABLE-BOOKING provider (Mamma Pizza now takes reservations) — the "book a table for two tomorrow at 8" flagship story, with a four-verb no-payment surface (availability/my_bookings queries, book_table/cancel_booking actions; `pay` absent from advertised capabilities), PoW + a reputation curve reframed as anti-reservation-scalping, and owner-scoped isolation; the suite's single RLS *showcase* moved to getgrocery's new `demo:rls` task.
- 2026-07-19: getgrocery boots out-of-box like its siblings — in dev/test it uses a placeholder Stripe key when none is set, so demo:setup/schema/isolation run with no payment config; only a real charge (demo:shop) needs a live key or stripe-mock.
- 2026-07-19: KYC attestations can now carry named anonymized `attributes` ({name: true} booleans, e.g. age_over_18/licence_a) alongside the binary `level`, so an action can gate on required attributes and reject with the new `kyc_required` (403) wire error without ever learning the underlying documents; demonstrated by the skooti `rent_motorcycle` gate.
- 2026-07-19: A provider can cap each bound assistant's payments through the core `config.spending_cap` pay-hook seam, so a human governs (or disables) an assistant's spend; the pay path rejects an over-cap charge with the new `spending_cap_exceeded` (403) error before the irreversible capture.
- 2026-07-19: Re-pinned the skill to v0.2.4 — the default `skill_url` and all seven demos now advertise `skill-v0.2.4.md` and its SHA-256, keeping the dual-check pin in sync with the re-versioned skill (terminology + three additive 0.3-feature notes).
- 2026-07-19: roles-from-IdP (indirect, via the bound human) — the account-binding link ceremony now carries the signed-in human's `user_idp` role onto the link row and sets the bound agent's `allowed_roles` from it (fresh key: linked with that role; rebind: adopts the new human's role), so a provider whose IdP reports roles provisions an assistant's role without the agent ever self-selecting it; role-less/single-role providers are unchanged. Demonstrated in `kiosk-demo-stylish`, now dual-audience: salon staff (owner/stylist) sourced from a role-carrying StubUserIdp, a role-gated `salon_calendar` query (owner: whole book + revenue; stylist: own chairs), plus `demo:roles` and a redteam beat proving the gate is un-bypassable.
- 2026-07-19: Renamed the salon-booking demo `kiosk-demo-saas-booking` → `kiosk-demo-stylish` — the service now carries the coined brand "stylish" (a hair-styling booking service) while the seeded salon stays "Combette on Park"; database names, module, CI matrix, and cross-demo references updated to match.
- 2026-07-19: Account-binding rebind now watermark-revokes the key's pre-link tokens, exactly as unlink does — a claim is a principal change, so the agent must re-login for a token under the new holder rather than keep authenticating as the old principal.
- 2026-07-19: New `kiosk-demo-tudu` — a NON-COMMERCE collaborative todo app proving the four-verb surface carries multi-user collaboration with NO payments: membership-based many-to-many access (not just owner-scoped rows), agent→agent list invites over `run` (single-use codes, pure app-layer, no spec change), per-agent attribution, and the first real use of the W5 `assistant_claimed` rebind hook to migrate a headless account's data to a human on linking. Full human web UI; wired into the CI demos matrix.
- 2026-07-19: New `kiosk-demo-philslist` — a NON-COMMERCE classifieds board proving Kiosk is not only for commerce: the same query/run/schema + account-binding wire with NO payment provider, so `pay` is absent from the advertised capabilities. Its headline is cross-owner WRITE denial on owned listings (edit/close → 403) and a multi-account household (two assistants on one account); wired into the CI demos matrix.
- 2026-07-19: Discovery no longer advertises AP2/payments when the provider serves no `pay` — agents.txt drops `Protocols: ap2`/`Payments: required` and agents.json omits its `payments` block for payment-less providers, so the standard surfaces stay consistent with the computed `capabilities`.
- 2026-07-19: The advertised wire version is now 0.2.0 — Kiosk::Protocol::API_VERSION and MIN_CLIENT bump to match the 0.2 protocol, so the Kiosk-API-Version/Kiosk-Min-Client headers and the agents.json api_version now report parity with the framework release.
- 2026-07-18: sweep fixes — the registration toll 402 now carries WWW-Authenticate: Kiosk-PoW like the wire-verb gate; removed the unused WellKnown agents_json_string/api_catalog_string spec-only wrappers; re-pinned skill v0.2.2 across the five demos and the default URL.
- 2026-07-09: Adopted the Kiosk 0.1 convergence process: repo constitution in CLAUDE.md (five rules) and a repo-wide CI workflow running every gem suite, demo happy-path/isolation/redteam flows, and e2e.
- 2026-07-09: Discovery `/.well-known/kiosk.json` now advertises `capabilities` as verb names (schema/query/run/pay) computed from the registry, replacing the static `[query,actions,ap2]`.
- 2026-07-09: Registration accepts an optional provider `assistant_creation` factory so hosts with a validated account model no longer 500 on agent sign-up.
- 2026-07-09: AP2 mandate verification now requires all six spec-mandated claims (id, user_id, agent_id, iss, iat, exp), not exp alone — a mandate missing id/iat is rejected.
- 2026-07-09: Corrected gem READMEs and the equihash PoW cost claims to shipped reality (kiosk-server shipped; equihash is the default; canonical N×PoW reputation wire; honest ~16 ms/few-KB verify).
- 2026-07-10: Registration contract switched to block-returns-id — assistant_creation returns the new record's id, so bigint and uuid apps both work; agents.public_key gains a partial UNIQUE index against double-register races.
- 2026-07-10: Demos self-provision an ephemeral dev signing key when none is set, so every demo runs out of the box.
- 2026-07-10: saas-booking's OAuth device-grant walkthrough reframed as the optional existing-account path.
- 2026-07-11: Auth surface reworked — registration_role is optional and role-less agents work end-to-end (no role claim, no role GUC); invalid/expired/revoked tokens get 401 instead of 500; the KYC endpoint honors the configured IdP; the device grant rejects undeclared roles; the advertised skill URL is the immutable versioned file.
- 2026-07-11: kiosk-core drops its unwired extension seams — CredentialBrokers, NotificationAdapter, and the Event value type — until a shipped consumer needs them.
- 2026-07-11: Demos re-pin the skill integrity hash to the immutable skill-v0.1.1.md (pin drift now fails the test-support suite in a local multi-repo checkout); every stub-PSP demo registers payment_setup; skooti's per-demo skill teaches the real Equihash 402 wire; saas-booking gets per-demo database names and drops the nonexistent CLI pitch.
- 2026-07-11: READMEs and CI aligned with shipped reality — Equihash default everywhere, opt-in-RLS wording, no unshipped rake tasks or fraud-detection claims; CI runs demo:schema on all five demos and redteam on saas-booking; dependabot points at the real bundler roots.
- 2026-07-11: Identity plane defined — the bundled kiosk-pop engine is the default agent-IdP so zero-config installs verify what they mint; the wire falls through to user_idp by verification result; KYC is agent-only; custom-adapter token issuance is deferred to the 0.2 seam.
- 2026-07-12: Mandate verification normalizes the principal check to string (bigint-PK hosts no longer 403 every mandate) and rejects cross-currency carts against an intent cap; the KYC endpoint returns 400 not 500 on a malformed body; the unimplemented `events` verb was removed (unknown verbs return a clean error); the auth-challenge TTL default now outlives the registration PoW solve window; the advertised default skill URL bumps to skill-v0.1.2.md.
- 2026-07-12: Demos re-pin the skill integrity hash to skill-v0.1.2.md; saas-booking's queries/actions now carry schema descriptions; skooti's narrative PoW references corrected to the shipped Equihash gate and the nonexistent kiosk-credentials-* KYC gem replaced with the real verified-attestation path.
- 2026-07-13: saas-booking's `_test` device-authorization fixture routes and its bin/demo device-grant walkthrough are retired — no test-only HTTP surface ships in a demo; the README now presents the mounted OAuth endpoints as dormant and defers human↔agent account-linking past 0.1.
- 2026-07-12: READMEs/gemspecs scrubbed of superseded design-spec section citations and the removed kiosk-credentials-* family; dropped the last "production-grade" overclaim; CI runs each demo task in its own invocation and gives getgrocery a dummy Stripe key so its boot guard passes.
- 2026-07-12: getgrocery's demo:shop self-starts a local stripe-mock when no Stripe key is present, so CI runs the full register→pay→confirm flow (including the off_session charge) secret-free — no test keys in CI.
- 2026-07-12: Payment is agent-only — an agentless caller (user_idp web/mobile session, no agent_id) now gets a clean 403 on POST /pay instead of a 500 deep in mandate verification; the hoteling demo drops its inert copy-paste KYC route (no key configured).
- 2026-07-12: WireController now returns 400 bad_request (not an uncaught 500) on a malformed wire body, and Equihash.verify returns false (not raises) on a malformed proof index — two parse/validate-outside-guard robustness gaps found by the line audit.
- 2026-07-12: foodelivery and hoteling demo skills teach the real Equihash 402 PoW wire (were instructing the retired Argon2id solver, so an agent following them could not register).
- 2026-07-12: Line-audit cleanup — removed orphaned demo bin/demo scaffolding and dead e2e device-verify fixtures; scrubbed PoW/reputation/redteam docstrings, gemspecs and demo before-after snapshots of retired-Argon2id and pre-retune wording so shipped docs match the shipped Equihash reality; added coverage for revoke, malformed bodies and other error paths.
- 2026-07-12: KYC attestation verification normalizes the subject check to string, so a bigint-PK host no longer 403s every KYC attestation — the same string-principal fix already applied to mandate verification, mirrored to its KycVerifier sibling.
- 2026-07-12: Fix wave — removed dead payment-adapter contract seams (Base#authorize/#refund, Stripe#refund, Mandate::Settlement, Result#query_id) and the demos' dead _test device-grant routes; the e2e harness now proves per-user app-layer isolation with a genuine cross-user negative case (Alice/Bob each see only their own rows); added AuthController and cuckoo target-path coverage; and scrubbed PoW/reputation/redteam/rls/demo docstrings, gemspecs, changelogs and the getgrocery worked example to shipped reality.
- 2026-07-14: Fix wave A (real bugs) — mandate verification now rejects a chain that omits a required amount field (an absent total/amount nil-coerced to 0 and settled a 0-cent order) and returns 403 not 500 for a keyless agent; register/login return 400 not 500 on a non-String public_key and the unauthenticated challenge store is bounded; the generated mandates migration rolls back cleanly (drops settlements before its FK parent); Equihash/Cuckoo verify return false rather than raise on malformed proofs; the Devise user-IdP adapter actually authenticates through the wire (reads the request Warden user); foodelivery's showcase walkthrough is fixed and asserts (CI now runs its settling demo:order); and the scooter-specific RentalTokenIssuer moved out of the neutral core into the skooti demo.
- 2026-07-14: Fix wave B (honesty/dead-surface tail, 84 rows across every gem) — docstrings, READMEs, CHANGELOGs, gemspecs, SKILL fragments and demo narratives across core/server/PoW/reputation/redteam/pay-stripe/rls/test-support/e2e/demos were reconciled to shipped reality (only-shipped-gem naming, live kiosk-pop IdP attribution, real schema/task inventories, the shipped SetupIntent Stripe model, the kiosk.tech origin), a batch of spec-only dead constants/fields/branches was removed, and small coverage gaps (Identity claims-nil, NullExecutor queued results, count-curve edge, tamper_token branches, spec-DSL matchers) were filled.
- 2026-07-14: foodelivery's pay is now bound to a placed order — a new confirm_order step gates on order-ownership and a settlement bound to that order (mirroring getgrocery's schedule_delivery), so a principal cannot confirm another's order; the demo's isolation flow now proves this cross-principal 403 instead of skipping it.
- 2026-07-14: Fix wave C (doc-sibling tail, 14 rows) — the residual after waves A/B was fixes that corrected the code but left a CHANGELOG/SKILL/before-after/sibling-comment carrying the old claim; those doc siblings were swept to shipped reality (non-Rails controller guards, devise auth path, cuckoo spec count, foodelivery confirm_order docs), two dead spec-only surfaces were dropped/marked (Protocol::WELL_KNOWN_PATH, Errors::QuotaExceeded), and coverage was added for the PaymentProviders::Base default and the redteam KYC path.
- 2026-07-14: Mandate verification now rejects a mandate omitting the spec-required currency (it previously slipped through to a NOT NULL 500 after partial persist); and the skooti CI row gates demo:kat so the moved RentalTokenIssuer's known-answer/firmware-vector test runs on the merge gate.
- 2026-07-14: Doc-truth sweep — shipped comments/gemspec/READMEs corrected to reality: only kiosk-pay-stripe/kiosk-user-idp-devise ship (no Auth0/WorkOS/kiosk-agent-idp-* adapters), the e2e harness signs the full intent→cart→payment trail, and the dead Integer-only-contradicting target branch in cuckoo.verify was removed.
- 2026-07-15: Native standards alignment (0.2) — kiosk-server now emits /agents.txt + /agents.json (agents.txt v1.0: Protocols: ap2, Authorization: agent-auth, Skills: <skill>) and /.well-known/agent-configuration from one discovery generator, with the six-verb contract carried in an x-kiosk extension; the bespoke kiosk.json is kept as a byte-identical derived alias.
- 2026-07-15: The two HTTP 402 gates are disambiguated at the header level — pow_required carries WWW-Authenticate: Kiosk-PoW and payment_setup_required carries WWW-Authenticate: Payment (method=ap2), the N-challenge list / setup pointer still in the body (additive, back-compatible).
- 2026-07-15: All five demos + the e2e harness now serve the native discovery surface (/agents.txt, /agents.json, /.well-known/agent-configuration) from the shipped DiscoveryController, and route /.well-known/kiosk.json through it too.
- 2026-07-15: Discovery now also emits an RFC 9727 api-catalog (/.well-known/api-catalog, application/linkset+json) of the live wire endpoints (schema tagged service-desc); the skill is re-versioned to skill-v0.1.3.md (captures the WWW-Authenticate note) and every demo re-pins its integrity hash.
- 2026-07-17: Account binding lands in kiosk-server: the RFC 8628 machinery is revived as the key-bound claim/link ceremonies — public-key device authorization with a session-authenticated verify page, possession-proof-gated token poll (BIND-POP), link-code mint/redeem and unlink with assistant_claimed/assistant_unlinked hooks — over a durable ActiveRecord store (migration 008, codes stored hashed); discovery gains /auth.md plus auth-md/binding-mode deltas across agents.txt/json, agent-configuration and kiosk.json (additive). Tokens stay kiosk-pop-only.
- 2026-07-18: The demos now walk the account-binding scenarios: saas-booking gains real Devise sessions and `demo:binding` — the claim ceremony driven through the live sign-in form + verify page, then the human-initiated link code and unlink; getgrocery gains `demo:claim` — an already-registered standalone assistant is re-bound to a seeded human account (agent_id stable, user_id remapped, old order not migrated) and pays a new order with that account's saved card.
